Book: Backyard Blacksmith

ConsumableFast

When read, increases the Melee weapon crafting skill. The lower the skill, the larger the gain. Single-use publication. Read only in the bunker. Lower permanent skill means faster reading and a larger reward: from 6 minutes and +15 for a beginner to 4 hours and +1 for an expert. All times are in-game; skill cap: 100. Temporary penalties do not improve these terms. Another order pauses reading; use again to resume. Progress percentage is saved; terms adjust if the skill increases. One copy is consumed only on successful completion, never at skill 100.
